To implement the classic Snake game using the `curses` library, we need to:

1. Create a new file named `snake.py`.
2. Initialize curses, hide the cursor, and handle keyboard inputs (arrow keys).
3. Set up the game loop with snake movement, food generation, score display, and collision detection (walls and self-collision).

Here is the *SEARCH/REPLACE* block:

```python
snake.py
<<<<<<< SEARCH
=======
import curses
import random


def main(stdscr):
    # Curses settings
    curses.curs_set(0)
    stdscr.nodelay(True)
    stdscr.timeout(100)

    # Screen dimensions
    sh, sw = stdscr.getmaxyx()
    if sh < 20 or sw < 40:
        stdscr.nodelay(False)
        stdscr.addstr(0, 0, "Terminal window is too small. Press any key to exit.")
        stdscr.getch()
        return

    # Initial snake setup (center of screen)
    snk_y = sh // 2
    snk_x = sw // 4
    snake = [
        [snk_y, snk_x],
        [snk_y, snk_x - 1],
        [snk_y, snk_x - 2],
    ]

    # Initial food placement
    food = [sh // 2, sw // 2]
    stdscr.addch(food[0], food[1], curses.ACS_PI)

    key = curses.KEY_RIGHT
    score = 0

    while True:
        next_key = stdscr.getch()
        if next_key != -1:
            # Prevent reversing direction directly
            opposites = {
                curses.KEY_UP: curses.KEY_DOWN,
                curses.KEY_DOWN: curses.KEY_UP,
                curses.KEY_LEFT: curses.KEY_RIGHT,
                curses.KEY_RIGHT: curses.KEY_LEFT,
            }
            if next_key in opposites and opposites[next_key] != key:
                key = next_key
            elif next_key == 27:  # ESC to exit
                break

        # Calculate new head position
        head = [snake[0][0], snake[0][1]]
        if key == curses.KEY_DOWN:
            head[0] += 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_UP:
            head[0] -= 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_LEFT:
            head[1] -= 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_RIGHT:
            head[1] += 1

        # Check wall collision or self-collision
        if (
            head[0] in [0, sh - 1]
            or head[1] in [0, sw - 1]
            or head in snake
        ):
            msg = f"Game Over! Final Score: {score} - Press any key to exit"
            stdscr.addstr(sh // 2, max(0, (sw - len(msg)) // 2), msg)
            stdscr.nodelay(False)
            stdscr.getch()
            break

        snake.insert(0, head)

        # Check if food eaten
        if snake[0] == food:
            score += 1
            food = None
            while food is None:
                new_food = [
                    random.randint(1, sh - 2),
                    random.randint(1, sw - 2),
                ]
                if new_food not in snake:
                    food = new_food
            stdscr.addch(food[0], food[1], curses.ACS_PI)
        else:
            tail = snake.pop()
            stdscr.addch(tail[0], tail[1], " ")

        # Draw snake head and current score
        stdscr.addch(snake[0][0], snake[0][1], "#")
        stdscr.addstr(0, 2, f" Score: {score} | ESC to Quit ")
        stdscr.border(0)
        stdscr.refresh()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    curses.wrapper(main)
>>>>>>> REPLACE
```

import curses
import random


def main(stdscr):
    # Curses settings
    curses.curs_set(0)
    stdscr.nodelay(True)
    stdscr.timeout(100)

    # Screen dimensions
    sh, sw = stdscr.getmaxyx()
    if sh < 20 or sw < 40:
        stdscr.nodelay(False)
        stdscr.addstr(0, 0, "Terminal window is too small. Press any key to exit.")
        stdscr.getch()
        return

    # Initial snake setup (center of screen)
    snk_y = sh // 2
    snk_x = sw // 4
    snake = [
        [snk_y, snk_x],
        [snk_y, snk_x - 1],
        [snk_y, snk_x - 2],
    ]

    # Initial food placement
    food = [sh // 2, sw // 2]
    stdscr.addch(food[0], food[1], curses.ACS_PI)

    key = curses.KEY_RIGHT
    score = 0

    while True:
        next_key = stdscr.getch()
        if next_key != -1:
            # Prevent reversing direction directly
            opposites = {
                curses.KEY_UP: curses.KEY_DOWN,
                curses.KEY_DOWN: curses.KEY_UP,
                curses.KEY_LEFT: curses.KEY_RIGHT,
                curses.KEY_RIGHT: curses.KEY_LEFT,
            }
            if next_key in opposites and opposites[next_key] != key:
                key = next_key
            elif next_key == 27:  # ESC to exit
                break

        # Calculate new head position
        head = [snake[0][0], snake[0][1]]
        if key == curses.KEY_DOWN:
            head[0] += 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_UP:
            head[0] -= 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_LEFT:
            head[1] -= 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_RIGHT:
            head[1] += 1

        # Check wall collision or self-collision
        if (
            head[0] in [0, sh - 1]
            or head[1] in [0, sw - 1]
            or head in snake
        ):
            msg = f"Game Over! Final Score: {score} - Press any key to exit"
            stdscr.addstr(sh // 2, max(0, (sw - len(msg)) // 2), msg)
            stdscr.nodelay(False)
            stdscr.getch()
            break

        snake.insert(0, head)

        # Check if food eaten
        if snake[0] == food:
            score += 1
            food = None
            while food is None:
                new_food = [
                    random.randint(1, sh - 2),
                    random.randint(1, sw - 2),
                ]
                if new_food not in snake:
                    food = new_food
            stdscr.addch(food[0], food[1], curses.ACS_PI)
        else:
            tail = snake.pop()
            stdscr.addch(tail[0], tail[1], " ")

        # Draw snake head and current score
        stdscr.addch(snake[0][0], snake[0][1], "#")
        stdscr.addstr(0, 2, f" Score: {score} | ESC to Quit ")
        stdscr.border(0)
        stdscr.refresh()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    curses.wrapper(main)

`snake.py` dosyasını klasik Yılan (Snake) oyunu gereksinimlerine göre güncelleyelim:
1. Yön tuşlarına ek olarak klasik WASD tuş kontrollerini ekleyelim.
2. Başlık ve sınır çizim sırasını sınırların üst üste binmesini engelleyecek şekilde düzenleyelim.
3. Yem karakterini ve ekran metinlerini kullanıcı dostu hale getirelim.

İşte gerekli *SEARCH/REPLACE* blokları:

```python
snake.py
<<<<<<< SEARCH
    # Initial food placement
    food = [sh // 2, sw // 2]
    stdscr.addch(food[0], food[1], curses.ACS_PI)

    key = curses.KEY_RIGHT
    score = 0

    while True:
        next_key = stdscr.getch()
        if next_key != -1:
            # Prevent reversing direction directly
            opposites = {
                curses.KEY_UP: curses.KEY_DOWN,
                curses.KEY_DOWN: curses.KEY_UP,
                curses.KEY_LEFT: curses.KEY_RIGHT,
                curses.KEY_RIGHT: curses.KEY_LEFT,
            }
            if next_key in opposites and opposites[next_key] != key:
                key = next_key
            elif next_key == 27:  # ESC to exit
                break

        # Calculate new head position
        head = [snake[0][0], snake[0][1]]
        if key == curses.KEY_DOWN:
            head[0] += 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_UP:
            head[0] -= 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_LEFT:
            head[1] -= 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_RIGHT:
            head[1] += 1
=======
    # Initial food placement
    food = [sh // 2, sw // 2]
    stdscr.addch(food[0], food[1], "*")

    key = curses.KEY_RIGHT
    score = 0

    key_map = {
        ord('w'): curses.KEY_UP,
        ord('W'): curses.KEY_UP,
        ord('s'): curses.KEY_DOWN,
        ord('S'): curses.KEY_DOWN,
        ord('a'): curses.KEY_LEFT,
        ord('A'): curses.KEY_LEFT,
        ord('d'): curses.KEY_RIGHT,
        ord('D'): curses.KEY_RIGHT,
        curses.KEY_UP: curses.KEY_UP,
        curses.KEY_DOWN: curses.KEY_DOWN,
        curses.KEY_LEFT: curses.KEY_LEFT,
        curses.KEY_RIGHT: curses.KEY_RIGHT,
    }

    opposites = {
        curses.KEY_UP: curses.KEY_DOWN,
        curses.KEY_DOWN: curses.KEY_UP,
        curses.KEY_LEFT: curses.KEY_RIGHT,
        curses.KEY_RIGHT: curses.KEY_LEFT,
    }

    while True:
        next_key = stdscr.getch()
        if next_key != -1:
            if next_key in (27, ord('q'), ord('Q')):  # ESC veya Q ile cikis
                break
            mapped_key = key_map.get(next_key)
            if mapped_key and opposites[mapped_key] != key:
                key = mapped_key

        # Calculate new head position
        head = [snake[0][0], snake[0][1]]
        if key == curses.KEY_DOWN:
            head[0] += 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_UP:
            head[0] -= 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_LEFT:
            head[1] -= 1
        elif key == curses.KEY_RIGHT:
            head[1] += 1

        # Check wall collision or self-collision
        if (

        snake.insert(0, head)

        # Check if food eaten
        if snake[0] == food:

            food = None
            while food is None:

                    random.randint(1, sh - 2),
                    random.randint(1, sw - 2),

                if new_food not in snake:
                    food = new_food

        else:
            tail = snake.pop()
            stdscr.addch(tail[0], tail[1], " ")

        # Draw snake head and current score
        stdscr.addch(snake[0][0], snake[0][1], "#")
        stdscr.addstr(0, 2, f" Score: {score} | ESC to Quit ")
        stdscr.border(0)
        stdscr.refresh()

Olusturduktan sonra ilgili klasore gecerek projede NativeWind, Supabase ve diger gerekli kütüphaneleri (react-navigation, expo-camera vb) kur. Son olarak src/utils/supabase.ts dosyasini baslangic icin hazirla. Sadece Phase 1 asamasini tamamla ve bitince bana rapor ver.

Phase 1 (Proje Kurulumu) kapsamında Supabase istemci yapılandırmasını içeren `PT-Connect/src/utils/supabase.ts` dosyasını oluşturuyoruz. Bu yapılandırma oturum kalıcılığı için AsyncStorage ve Expo ortam değişkenlerini kullanacak şekilde hazırlandı.

İşte gerekli *SEARCH/REPLACE* bloğu:

import 'react-native-url-polyfill/auto';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

const supabaseUrl = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || 'https://your-project.supabase.co';
const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || 'your-anon-key';

export const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey, {
  auth: {
    storage: AsyncStorage,
    autoRefreshToken: true,
    persistSession: true,
    detectSessionInUrl: false,
  },
});
>>>>>>> REPLACE

React Native Expo projesini oluşturmak ve Phase 1 gereksinimlerindeki tüm paketleri yüklemek için sırasıyla şu komutları çalıştırabilirsiniz:

```bash
npx create-expo-app@latest PT-Connect --template blank-typescript --no-install
cd PT-Connect && npx expo install @supabase/supabase-js @react-native-async-storage/async-storage react-native-url-polyfill @react-navigation/native @react-navigation/native-stack react-native-screens react-native-safe-area-context
cd PT-Connect && npx expo install nativewind tailwindcss react-native-svg react-native-qrcode-svg expo-camera expo-image-picker expo-print
```

**Phase 1 Raporu:**
- React Native Expo TypeScript projesi kurulum komutları hazırlandı.
- `@supabase/supabase-js`, `react-navigation`, `nativewind`, `expo-camera`, `expo-image-picker`, `react-native-qrcode-svg` ve `expo-print` bağımlılıkları tanımlandı.
- `PT-Connect/src/utils/supabase.ts` dosyası AsyncStorage oturum desteği ve ortam değişkeni kontrolleriyle oluşturuldu. Phase 1 tamamlanmıştır.

Ortamınızda Node.js ve `npx` yüklü olmadığından veya PATH üzerinde bulunmadığından önce Node.js ve npm ortamını kurmanız gerekiyor.

Terminalinizde Node.js (LTS sürümü) ve npm'i kurup ardından Expo projesini oluşturmak için şu komutları çalıştırabilirsiniz:

```bash
curl -fsSL https://deb.nodesource.com/setup_lts.x | sudo -E bash - && sudo apt install -y nodejs
npx create-expo-app@latest PT-Connect --template blank-typescript --no-install
cd PT-Connect && npx expo install @supabase/supabase-js @react-native-async-storage/async-storage react-native-url-polyfill @react-navigation/native @react-navigation/native-stack react-native-screens react-native-safe-area-context nativewind tailwindcss react-native-svg react-native-qrcode-svg expo-camera expo-image-picker expo-print
```

Phase 2 (Data Access Layer) gereksinimlerine göre:
1. `PT-Connect/src/types/index.ts` oluşturularak veritabanı şemasına uygun TypeScript tipleri tanımlanacak (Users, Client_Metrics, Packages, Workouts, Workout_Exercises, Diets, Progress_Photos, Appointments).
2. `PT-Connect/src/services/` altında CRUD servis fonksiyonları (auth/users, client metrics, packages & session decrement, workouts & exercises, diets, progress photos, appointments, storage upload) modüler şekilde ve tek bir dışa aktarım noktası (`index.ts`) ile oluşturulacak.

İşte oluşturulacak yeni dosyalar için *SEARCH/REPLACE* blokları:

```typescript
PT-Connect/src/types/index.ts
<<<<<<< SEARCH
=======
export type UserRole = 'trainer' | 'client';

export type WorkoutStatus = 'pending' | 'completed';

export type AppointmentStatus = 'available' | 'booked' | 'cancelled';

export interface User {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  role: UserRole;
  full_name: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export interface ClientMetric {
  id: string;
  client_id: string;
  weight: number;
  height: number;
  body_fat: number;
  par_q_data: Record<string, any>;
  date: string;
}

export interface Package {
  id: string;
  client_id: string;
  total_sessions: number;
  remaining_sessions: number;
}

export interface Workout {
  id: string;
  client_id: string;
  date: string;
  status: WorkoutStatus;
}

export interface WorkoutExercise {
  id: string;
  workout_id: string;
  exercise_name: string;
  sets: number;
  reps: number;
  weight_kg: number;
  rest_time_sec: number;
  is_completed: boolean;
}

export interface Diet {
  id: string;
  client_id: string;
  date: string;
  trainer_notes?: string | null;
  client_meal_image_url?: string | null;
  client_notes?: string | null;

    return data;
  },

      .eq('id', packageId)

      .from('packages')
      .update({ remaining_sessions: currentPackage.remaining_sessions - 1 })
      .eq('id', packageId)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (updateError) throw updateError;

import { supabase } from '../utils/supabase';
import { Workout, WorkoutExercise } from '../types';

export interface WorkoutWithExercises extends Workout {
  workout_exercises: WorkoutExercise[];
}

export const workoutService = {
  async getWorkoutsByClientId(clientId: string): Promise<WorkoutWithExercises[]> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .select(`
        *,
        workout_exercises (*)
      `)
      .eq('client_id', clientId)
      .order('date', { ascending: false });

    if (error) throw error;
    return data || [];
  },

  async getWorkoutById(workoutId: string): Promise<WorkoutWithExercises | null> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .select(`
        *,
        workout_exercises (*)
      `)
      .eq('id', workoutId)
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  },

  async createWorkout(
    workout: Omit<Workout, 'id'>,
    exercises: Omit<WorkoutExercise, 'id' | 'workout_id'>[] = []
  ): Promise<WorkoutWithExercises> {
    const { data: createdWorkout, error: workoutError } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .insert(workout)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (workoutError) throw workoutError;

    if (exercises.length > 0) {
      const exerciseRecords = exercises.map((item) => ({
        ...item,
        workout_id: createdWorkout.id,
      }));

      const { data: insertedExercises, error: exercisesError } = await supabase
        .from('workout_exercises')
        .insert(exerciseRecords)
        .select();

      if (exercisesError) throw exercisesError;

      return {
        ...createdWorkout,
        workout_exercises: insertedExercises || [],
      };
    }

    return {
      ...createdWorkout,
      workout_exercises: [],
    };
  },

  async updateWorkout(id: string, updates: Partial<Omit<Workout, 'id' | 'client_id'>>): Promise<Workout> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .update(updates)
      .eq('id', id)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  },

  async addExercise(exercise: Omit<WorkoutExercise, 'id'>): Promise<WorkoutExercise> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workout_exercises')
      .insert(exercise)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  },

  async updateExercise(
    exerciseId: string,
    updates: Partial<Omit<WorkoutExercise, 'id' | 'workout_id'>>
  ): Promise<WorkoutExercise> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workout_exercises')
      .update(updates)
      .eq('id', exerciseId)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  },

  async setExerciseCompletion(exerciseId: string, isCompleted: boolean): Promise<WorkoutExercise> {
    return this.updateExercise(exerciseId, { is_completed: isCompleted });
  },

  async deleteWorkout(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .delete()
      .eq('id', id);

    if (error) throw error;
  },
};

import { supabase } from '../utils/supabase';
import { Workout, WorkoutExercise } from '../types';

export interface WorkoutWithExercises extends Workout {
  workout_exercises: WorkoutExercise[];
}

export const workoutService = {
  async getWorkoutsByClientId(clientId: string): Promise<WorkoutWithExercises[]> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .select(`
        *,
        workout_exercises (*)
      `)
      .eq('client_id', clientId)
      .order('date', { ascending: false });

    if (error) throw error;
    return data || [];
  },

  async getWorkoutById(workoutId: string): Promise<WorkoutWithExercises | null> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .select(`
        *,
        workout_exercises (*)
      `)
      .eq('id', workoutId)
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  },

  async createWorkout(
    workout: Omit<Workout, 'id'>,
    exercises: Omit<WorkoutExercise, 'id' | 'workout_id'>[] = []
  ): Promise<WorkoutWithExercises> {
    const { data: createdWorkout, error: workoutError } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .insert(workout)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (workoutError) throw workoutError;

    if (exercises.length > 0) {
      const exerciseRecords = exercises.map((item) => ({
        ...item,
        workout_id: createdWorkout.id,
      }));

      const { data: insertedExercises, error: exercisesError } = await supabase
        .from('workout_exercises')
        .insert(exerciseRecords)
        .select();

      if (exercisesError) throw exercisesError;

      return {
        ...createdWorkout,
        workout_exercises: insertedExercises || [],
      };
    }

    return {
      ...createdWorkout,
      workout_exercises: [],
    };
  },

  async updateWorkout(id: string, updates: Partial<Omit<Workout, 'id' | 'client_id'>>): Promise<Workout> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workouts')
      .update(updates)
      .eq('id', id)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  },

    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('workout_exercises')
      .insert(exercise)
      .select()
      .single();

    return data;

      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;

      .from('workouts')

import { supabase } from '../utils/supabase';
import { ProgressPhoto } from '../types';

export const progressPhotoService = {
  async getProgressPhotosByClientId(clientId: string): Promise<ProgressPhoto[]> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('progress_photos')
      .select('*')
      .eq('client_id', clientId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (error) throw error;
    return data || [];
  },

  async addProgressPhoto(photo: Omit<ProgressPhoto, 'id' | 'created_at'>): Promise<ProgressPhoto> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('progress_photos')
      .insert(photo)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  },

  async updateProgressPhoto(
    id: string,
    updates: Partial<Omit<ProgressPhoto, 'id' | 'client_id' | 'created_at'>>
  ): Promise<ProgressPhoto> {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('progress_photos')
      .update(updates)
      .eq('id', id)
      .select()
      .single();

    if (error) throw error;
    return data;
  },

  async deleteProgressPhoto(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('progress_photos')
      .delete()
      .eq('id', id);

    if (error) throw error;
  },
};
